# Screening Inspection

- **Review:** Non‐invasive diagnostic tests for   Helicobacter pylori infection
- **Review URL:** https://www.cochranelibrary.com/cdsr/doi/10.1002/14651858.CD012080.pub2/full
- **Screening criteria:** We included diagnostic accuracy studies that evaluated at least one of the index tests (urea breath test using isotopes such as 13C or 14C, serology and stool antigen test) against the reference standard (histopathological examination using H & E stain, special stains or immunohistochemical stain) in people suspected of having H pylori infection.
- **Gold label policy:** `include_label_0_5`
- **Gold screening rule:** dataset labels 1.0 and 0.5 count as INCLUDE; 0.0 counts as EXCLUDE.
- **Matches:** 61 / 1271
- **Mismatches:** 39
- **Missing decisions:** 1171
